# KONOSUBA Humor Mechanics Ledger — Through V16

## Core V01 mechanisms

| Pattern | Representative V01 scene | Mechanism | Modeling consequence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Heroic setup → mundane humiliation | Kazuma's “sacrifice” / tractor-shock death | anti-heroic inversion, bathos | motive can remain real even if outcome is gag-amplified |
| High-status role → petty behavior | goddess Aqua mocks dead Kazuma | status incongruity | do not equate divine role with emotional maturity |
| High specs → self-sabotage | Aqua/Megumin/Darkness builds | failed competence, incompatible objective functions | capacity and judgment/preference must be separate |
| Repetition with escalated consequence | Aqua swallowed by frogs twice | recurrence, non-update, status escalation | repeated mechanism supports model more than one gag |
| Serious role-language → absurd content | Megumin introduction | register incongruity | voice/register is part of humor engine |
| “Useful skill” expectation → underwear | Steal | sexual embarrassment, random-system reversal | Kazuma's handling is more diagnostic than random result |
| Monster/hero category reversal | Wiz; Kyouya | genre expectation violation | Kazuma often privileges observed conduct over category |
| Villain becomes victim | Beldia's castle repeatedly bombed | perspective reversal | Megumin/Aqua can create crisis while seeing themselves as justified |
| Maximal success → collateral disaster | Aqua flood | competence inversion | Aqua failure is often uncontrolled success, not inability |
| Boss dignity → soccer ball | Beldia head theft | status degradation + opportunistic escalation | H2: portable ruthless improvisation, nonportable magnitude |
| Victory reward → bigger debt | epilogue | delayed reversal, causal callback | comedy preserves material consequences rather than resetting |

## H1-H4 calibration examples

### H1 — direct behavioral evidence
- Aqua spends expected cabbage income before earning it.
- Megumin explicitly refuses non-Explosion spells.
- Darkness explicitly refuses accuracy investment.
- Kazuma observes water behavior and builds tactic.

### H2 — real disposition with comic amplification
- frog swallowing and Aqua's repeated status-driven rematch;
- Kazuma turning Beldia's head into soccer;
- Darkness's battlefield sexualization of armor damage;
- Aqua's flood-scale response to insult.

### H3 — gag-contingent / weak generalization
- exact random sequence of underwear thefts through Steal.
- incidental physical positioning used only for one punchline.

### H4 — nonliteral comic license
V01 has relatively little that should be treated as wholly nonliteral because the fantasy rules support many extreme events. The strongest H4 candidates are cartoon-like physical/emotional magnitudes rather than the underlying motives themselves.

## Seriousness-interruption function

V01 repeatedly lets a gag pattern continue into high stakes and then reveals its boundary:
- Darkness enjoys danger yet refuses to retreat while protecting others.
- Kazuma is cynical until actual people are cut down.
- Megumin performs grandiosity until guilt over Darkness's curse.
- Aqua's comic unreliability coexists with genuine resurrection of the dead.

This “joke stops / mechanism remains” boundary is a primary reconstruction target.

## Emerging V01 humor thesis

Most humor is not random absurdity. It is generated by **stable but incompatible reward functions** colliding with a world whose mechanics are literal enough for bad decisions to create real downstream costs.

## H-class refinements from V02

### H1
- party-swap performance;
- Aqua's cemetery shortcut and apology;
- Kazuma's daily grave care;
- Darkness's explicit civic duty;
- Aqua's Keele rite;
- Kazuma's two Steal physical-consequence errors.

### H2
- resurrection reunion ending in “change”;
- Haunted-house physical panic magnitude;
- Aqua premature victory / narrative jinx;
- Darkness's extreme humiliation response after Destroyer.

### H3/H4 caution
The bath/succubus sequence cannot be transferred literally into a scenario where Kazuma knows he is awake and understands the encounter is real. The behavioral value lies in his prior privacy precautions, false appraisal, sexual reward structure, and subsequent trust behavior.

## Updated humor thesis
V02 strengthens the idea that KONOSUBA humor is **causal**: extreme preferences and partial competencies interact with literal fantasy rules, then consequences persist. The best gags often arise when an action is simultaneously intelligent along one dimension and foolish along another.

## V03 humor-system synthesis

V03 strengthens the hypothesis that KONOSUBA comedy is frequently generated by **correct local reasoning inside a bizarre objective function** rather than by generalized stupidity. Institutional systems and outsider observers make the mismatch visible.

The multi-POV epilogue adds a second layer: self-serving causal accounting is itself a recurrent joke engine. Aqua and Megumin can each privately view themselves as the party's responsible member because each excludes a different protected blind spot from their accounting.

Major V04 comedy remains predominantly L0-L2. The funny mechanisms usually survive translation because they depend on causal reversal, expectation, social scripts, or register. Japanese materially improves timing and interpersonal stance but L3 wordplay is not the dominant engine.


# V05 Humor-Mechanics Update

## 1. Core volume pattern: competence and absurd objective functions
V05 broadens the series' comedy from "party members have distorted priorities" to an entire culture where technical competence and ridiculous aesthetic priorities coexist. Crimson Demon adults are elite spellcasters while treating theatrical names, dramatic entrances, tourist traps, and contrived role identities as ordinary social practice.

**Mechanism:** expectation that ridiculous self-presentation predicts incompetence -> repeated inversion through genuine excellence.

## 2. Tranquility Girl — social-script predation
- **Setup:** helpless lonely-girl appearance activates empathy/protection.
- **Mechanism:** audience/Kazuma know category danger but immediate social script resists killing.
- **Reveal:** exact plea is repeated to a new victim, then predator complains in ordinary language.
- **Humor:** dramatic irony + social-script exploitation + sudden de-anthropomorphizing evidence.
- **Evidence class:** H1 for Kazuma decision updating; H2/H3 for exaggerated presentation.

## 3. Female-orc reversal
- **Setup:** Kazuma imports standard fantasy assumptions about orcs.
- **Reversal:** local sex/gender ecology makes him the prey.
- **Function:** exposes schema anchoring and creates a genuine fear-state relationship test with Aqua/Yunyun.
- **Evidence:** do not transfer the cartoon sexual-threat mechanics literally; preserve Kazuma's intense fear and Aqua's serious comforting response.

## 4. Crimson Demon declarations
The declarations are not merely boke behavior. They are a **cultural performance genre** shared by highly competent people. Jokes arise from:
- massive mismatch between register and mundane activity;
- outsiders imitating it;
- Yunyun refusing it despite belonging to the culture;
- Darkness failing it through name/status embarrassment;
- serious battle making the performance socially meaningful.

## 5. Tourism / lost technology
Sacred sword, wishing spring, ancient facility, weapon-as-clothesline and related sites combine:
- fantasy reverence;
- scam tourism;
- Earth technology misrecognition;
- reader dramatic irony.
Mostly L0-L2 rather than L3.

## 6. Kazuma's romantic/status failure loops
V05 uses attraction as an attention distortion and grievance source. The portable behavioral evidence is that Kazuma becomes more status-sensitive, impulsive, and self-justifying when intimacy/attraction is salient. Do not transfer specific sexual-comedy setups literally into cross-context simulation.

## 7. Megumin peer bragging
Megumin weaponizes technically true but implication-heavy relationship facts against classmates. Humor comes from selective truth + audience inference + competitive status, not from social naivete.

## 8. Final Chapter reversal
**Expected optimization:** Megumin finally learns practical advanced magic after five volumes of complaints.

**Reversal:** Kazuma, the person who most complained, secretly invests all saved points in stronger Explosion.

The joke succeeds because the relationship model has changed. It simultaneously resolves character growth:
- Megumin proves she can choose group utility over identity;
- Kazuma proves he values her identity over the utility he demanded.

**Evidence class:** H1 for underlying choices/relationship; H2 for extreme game-build framing.

## 9. Sylvia body/gender-category reveal
The source uses a period-specific sexual-category panic joke around Sylvia's chimera body. For current analytical use:
- treat the portable evidence as Kazuma's rigid attraction schema colliding with unexpected information;
- treat the exact gag framing as culturally/period-sensitive H3 rather than a general moral or identity claim;
- do not reproduce derogatory framing in reconstruction outputs.

## 10. V05 L-level distribution
Major mechanisms remain dominated by:
- **L0:** situation/reversal, role routing, status, character motive;
- **L1:** timing/wording emphasis;
- **L2:** cultural performance, social register, fantasy/Japanese gamer conventions;
- **L3:** present but not dominant.

This strongly supports MG01-J02 through a second prospective volume.


# V06 Update

## High-value V06 mechanisms

1. **Technically true heroic reframing (H1/H2, L1-L2):** Kazuma's adventure stories remain rooted in real events while motive/status emphasis is strategically altered.
2. **Role inversion (H2/H3):** the low-level "weak" Kazuma becomes a terrifying masked castle invader because beginner skills are sequenced adversarially.
3. **Competence misallocation (H1/H2):** Aqua performs astonishing court/shop art while forgetting the instrumental commercial objective.
4. **Status collision (H1/H2):** Darkness's impeccable noble role coexists with friends who know and publicly deploy `Lalatina` as a genuine aversion.
5. **Masked-identity dramatic irony (H1/H2):** Megumin admires the mysterious masked thief without recognizing Kazuma; Kazuma later wants credit for his own alter ego.
6. **Body-swap power fantasy/reversal (H2/H3/H4):** royal deference rapidly degrades Kazuma's restraint; the swap ends at maximum expected payoff and returns him into the consequences of Iris/Megumin's street behavior.
7. **Institutional reputational reversal (H1/H2):** Kazuma is publicly treated as useless for dying to kobolds while his thief alter ego is admired for defeating the same elites who dismiss him.
8. **Return-to-baseline domestic bathos (H1):** after national crisis, royal separation, artifact theft, and future-marriage symbolism, the party immediately resumes alcohol, snack, teasing, and age-status arguments at home.

## L0-L3 assessment
The principal humor remains situation, role, causal reversal, status, rhythm, and pragmatic register. Japanese-specific language adds precision—especially address and formal/informal shifts—but L3 wordplay is still a minority mechanism. MG01-J02 is confirmed across V04-V06.

## Comic-license boundary
The body-swap bath escalation should not be transferred literally into grounded scenarios. The useful reconstruction evidence is the **direction** of Kazuma's response to asymmetric power, sexual opportunity, and delayed accountability. Literal timing/intensity is H3-amplified.

# V07 Update

## Major V07 humor mechanisms

### HM-V07-01 — Heroic action immediately punctured by stable vice
Kazuma pays twenty billion, crashes a wedding and rescues Darkness; minutes later he hides behind Darkness from Megumin's Explosion. The joke preserves attachment and cowardice simultaneously rather than allowing a heroic scene to replace his normal model. **H2/H3 boundary.**

### HM-V07-02 — Sacred competence destroys melodrama
Darkness/father reach a near-death emotional farewell; Aqua instantly removes the demonic curse. The comedy is a causal reversal produced by genuine extreme competence, not incompetence. **H1 + structural L0.**

### HM-V07-03 — Masochistic relief contaminates rescue language
Kazuma's coercive-sounding `身体で払ってもらう` is intended as adventurer labor/debt rhetoric; Darkness's masochistic/sexual interpretation produces an immediate bodily reaction. Underlying relief, attraction and masochistic disposition are real; magnitude is comic. **H2.**

### HM-V07-04 — The "bad wizard" is the hero
Megumin adopts the villainous bride-stealing role in a genuinely prosocial rescue. Role inversion creates humor and grandeur simultaneously. **H1/H2, L0-L2.**

### HM-V07-05 — Friendship scarcity as tactical currency
Megumin can recruit Yunyun into criminal danger by calling her `我が親友`, then immediately resume abrasive denial/asymmetry. The joke depends on established relationship reward structure. **H1/H2, L1-L2.**

### HM-V07-06 — Legal status becomes a new teasing weapon
Interrupted wedding plus filed marriage paperwork yields the possibility that Darkness is technically divorced; Kazuma creates `バツネス`. This is a smaller L3-ish lexical joke embedded in a larger L0-L2 relationship scene.

### HM-V07-07 — Aqua's dragon egg
The joke works because Aqua's prestige/rarity/caretaker incentives are psychologically coherent enough to sustain an obviously false interpretation. **H2** rather than pure nonliteral gag.

## Humor evidence conclusion through V07
Major V07 comedy remains dominated by causal reversal, role collision, status, pragmatic timing and relationship scripts. Japanese-form-dependent L3 material exists but is not the principal engine. This strengthens MG02-J02.

# V08 Update

## H1 - direct behavioral evidence
- Aqua explicitly learns the "getting carried away causes trouble" rule and successfully applies it during insect clearing.
- Kazuma explicitly proposes using Axis/Eris rivalry to increase festival scale and funding.
- Megumin fires Explosion after being told there is no safe forest role for it.
- Darkness's public stage embarrassment becomes genuine anger rather than erotic reward.
- Eris continues responding to worshipper requests beyond a safe crowd threshold.

## H2 - stable mechanism plus comic amplification
- Aqua's worship hunger escalates into increasingly absurd festival supremacy behavior.
- Kazuma's advisor pride turns legitimate event competence into sexualized/profiteering scope creep.
- Darkness's ant experience moves from distress into masochistic reappraisal.
- Aigis mirrors some of Kazuma's sexualized appraisal in exaggerated object form.

## H3 - gag-contingent
- police/arrest recurrence;
- birthday-custom fakeout;
- exact crowd chanting and costume-contest escalation;
- some Aigis verbal bits.

## H4 - nonliteral/comic license
- physical survivability of certain Explosion shockwave/crowd incidents;
- cartoon riot magnitude;
- some exaggerated body-comedy timing.

## V08 system-level humor
The dominant engine is **successful local optimization producing a system-level absurdity**:
- rivalry -> bigger festival -> factional escalation;
- healing discipline -> still gets punished by someone else's Explosion;
- useful tanking -> ants in armor;
- public Eris recognition -> dangerous worshipper crowd;
- beauty contest -> both solves Aigis and triggers riot;
- worship success -> Aqua's worse behavior.

## Translation dependence
V08 remains overwhelmingly L0-L2. Japanese register/pragmatics sharpen character voice, but central jokes survive as causal/status/role structures rather than script-only L3 devices.


# V09 Update

**Evidence boundary:** Japanese V09 main narrative only. Locators below identify OEBPS/Text/part00XX.xhtml and its ordinal body paragraph P####, including blank/image-only paragraphs in the count. These findings add V09 evidence without rewriting earlier model history.

## HM-V09-01 — A shared specialty creates both care and inconvenience

The opening Explosion appraisal presents Kazuma and Megumin as participants in an established evaluative ritual. He awards a score, she accepts it as feedback, and he carries her home; she then explains that each improvement is being reinvested into greater spell expenditure, so level growth will not remove the transport burden. Her claim that shared contact matters is simultaneously affectionate and convenient for her. The joke does not require either the attachment or the imposed inconvenience to be false. **H1** for the investment choice, continuing transport, and stated relational valuation; **H2** for the grandiose sommelier/audience presentation. **L0–L2.** (part0009.xhtml P0009–0028.)

The charm-making sequence similarly places sincere protective care beside abrasive household behavior. Megumin makes charms for everyone and identifies the most durable one with Darkness's protective work. Kazuma keeps his gift close rather than casually putting it in his luggage. The private quarrel occurring while the others discuss lasting companionship gives the reader two simultaneous versions of the same household: real care and ridiculous conduct. **H1** for gifts and response; **H2** for the staged juxtaposition. (part0010.xhtml P0061–0088, P0343–0348, P0370–0394.)

## HM-V09-02 — Successful restraint produces a chain of mundane failures

The Bind sequence begins with Darkness's genuine request, but successful binding creates practical dependence that neither participant manages well. Concealment to protect appearances worsens those appearances; attempts to solve the resulting inconvenience become another occasion for score-settling. Kazuma repeatedly understands a local problem while allowing grievance or anticipated embarrassment to obstruct the remedy. The eventual explanation is accepted rapidly by Megumin, which exposes how much of the escalation depended on their predictions about being seen. (part0010.xhtml P0184–0227, P0248–0281, P0295–0341, P0395–0485.)

**H1:** Darkness's specific preference, changing distress, explicit refusals, requests for help, and Kazuma's choices to delay or intervene. **H2:** the prolonged escalation and polarized embarrassment. **H3:** the exact bodily/comic timing; it should not become a general simulation script. Darkness's earlier participation does not make every later inconvenience or exposure welcome. Her own distinctions, including the rejection of the proposed bottle solution and the statement that she cannot currently enjoy the situation, control that inference. (part0010.xhtml P0363–0365, P0417–0433.) Predominantly **L0–L2**, with a small written-language effect in the charm/babysitting wording recorded in the voice ledger.

## HM-V09-03 — Competition rewards the person who ignores the competition's frame

Kazuma wins the nominal contest by taking targets before Aqua can reach them; the resulting pursuit makes all three lose attention to the surrounding enemies. A sincere recovery of complementary party roles follows. Megumin then overrides her referee role, takes the whole target, and claims the strongest-member position, blowing away allies along with enemies. The punchline interrupts Kazuma's cooperative speech rather than simply confirming it. (part0011.xhtml P0172–0198, P0214–0271.)

**H1:** incentives, target choices, role abandonment, and the explicit claim to take the conspicuous success. **H2/H3:** the spectacular interruption and exact injury/recovery presentation. Darkness is left unconscious in the immediate scene; this consequence must remain visible. No inference of uniformly harmless slapstick is justified. The scene limits any rule that growing affection automatically makes Megumin cautious about companions. It is not a clean substitute for the frozen M04 test of the best tactical use directly causing serious companion harm, but it is counterevidence to a broader automatic inhibition rule. **L0–L1.**

## HM-V09-04 — Valuable expertise is hidden by the job's prestige

Aqua's construction work stops the siege from unfolding as a simple duel narrative. Skilled repair, accelerated drying, an organized team, and a satisfying title make the wall grow stronger despite repeated bombardment. The threatened fortress becomes confident enough for celebration, while Megumin and Yunyun are left with personal conflicts that the military problem no longer requires them to resolve immediately. (part0014.xhtml P0047–0110.)

**H1:** demonstrated repair, persistence across days, role assignment, and strategic effect. **H2:** the exuberant captain chorus and the enemy's indignant inspection of the improved wall. **L0–L2.** Recognition is productive here because it rewards the task itself. Aqua's pride need not compete with useful performance; the relevant question is what earns the pride. Her decorative work is reported only after repair and extension have restored safety, so it should not automatically be scored as loss of the main objective. (part0014.xhtml P0083–0110, P0116–0127.)

## HM-V09-05 — Mirrored tactics turn ordinary practice into frightening power

The enemy's Explosion-and-Teleport tactic is adopted against its own encampment. What resembles Megumin's familiar daily activity becomes a coordinated military operation through Kazuma's concealment/reconnaissance and Yunyun's extraction, while defenders handle the resulting counterattack. Repetition then changes the enemy's reactions from confidence to panic and pleading. Yunyun explicitly questions which side now resembles the Demon King's army. (part0014.xhtml P0199–0217, P0228–0258, P0260–0316.)

**H1:** successful role coordination and Megumin's increasing attention to efficient experience gain; her final complaint and Kazuma's correction directly establish goal drift. **H2:** the accumulating chorus of enemy excuses, pleas, and expectations. The enemies' speech provides individualizing cues within the comedy; do not erase these cues by reducing the sequence to a mechanically neutral level-gain montage. The passage does not establish the truth of Kazuma's speculation about why the surviving army cannot withdraw. **L0–L2.** (part0014.xhtml P0286–0321.)

## HM-V09-06 — The rejected prop becomes the emergency resource

Kazuma's mock Explosion performance provokes Megumin because it appropriates the identity and prestige of her specialty, even though she recognizes the device might be useful. Aqua's unsolicited miniaturization initially appears to ruin preparation; the small item that remains in Kazuma's pocket later creates the interruption needed for escape. The payoff preserves both the earlier apparent defect and the later contingent utility. (part0011.xhtml P0520–0536, P0569–0617; part0014.xhtml P0383–0390, P0402–0410.)

**H1:** identity defense, possession, and eventual use. **H2:** performance escalation and the delayed usefulness reversal. **L0–L2.** The source does not establish that shrinking increased the item's explosive force, nor that Aqua foresaw its eventual use; neither should be credited retrospectively.

## HM-V09-07 — Recognized roles fail to command the expected respect

Bandits are treated as a rare encounter worth admiring, while Darkness's proud announcement of noble identity frightens them away before her desired script can proceed. Their retreat is explained through recognized danger, not a victory in direct combat. (part0012.xhtml P0099–0138.) Later, the noble-title performance that secures entry to the fortress simultaneously embarrasses Darkness and lets her companions retaliate against their earlier mistreatment. (part0012.xhtml P0512–0544.) **H1/H2, L0–L2.**

The divine dispute likewise turns on the mismatch between true standing and persuasive self-presentation: Aqua can perceive divinity while refusing Wolbach's preferred classification, and Wolbach doubts Aqua's claim until power is displayed. Threatened punishments are deflated by the target's ordinary habits. The subsequent flood has real tactical costs, so the comic dispute cannot be detached from the failed operation. (part0013.xhtml P0256–0300; part0014.xhtml P0016–0022.) **H1/H2, L0–L2.**

## HM-V09-08 — Romantic competence fails at the level of particulars

Kazuma's confidence as an observer of romantic fiction does not supply confidence when affection is directed at him. His attempt to name what he likes defaults to Explosion, and Megumin recognizes the formula instead of accepting it as adequate personal understanding. Her own explanation of attraction is detailed, unidealized, and funny partly because he initially hears the listed flaws as criticism rather than affection. (part0014.xhtml P0616–0624, P0719–0740.)

The sequence does not end solely through an external interruption: after the interruption fails to separate them, Megumin's tears change Kazuma's behavior and the scene becomes disclosure and consolation. Humor then returns through his embarrassing self-comparison and continued desire. **H1** for disclosures, distress response, and deferred escalation; **H2/H3** for romantic pacing and performative self-description. **L0–L2.** (part0014.xhtml P0685–0719, P0752–0815.)

## H4 and translation calibration

No load-bearing V09 motive claim above requires an event to be classified as wholly nonliteral H4. Supernatural repair, restraint, and destructive magic operate as causal facts within this novel. Exact comic magnitude and bodily timing receive H2/H3 limits where appropriate; their fantasy status alone is not grounds for discarding them.

Major comic sequences remain predominantly situational, causal, status-based, and pragmatic. Two local form-sensitive effects deserve explicit preservation: the different readings of the written charm/babysitting term, and the additive particle used in the recognition inference. The latter is especially consequential to the emotional resolution, but is not a major comic sequence and therefore does not by itself overturn MG02-J02's narrower prediction about humor. (part0010.xhtml P0061, P0116; part0014.xhtml P0179, P0537.)


# V10 Update

**Evidence boundary:** verified Japanese V10 main narrative only. Locator map: Pr = `text/part0009.html`; C1 = `part0010.html`; C2 = `part0011.html`; C3 = `part0012.html`; C4 = `part0013.html`; C5 = `part0014.html`; Ep = `part0015.html`, all under `text/`. P numbers count every body paragraph, including blanks/images; only text was inspected. V10 is the first volume of the still-open V10–V12 tranche, with Gen03 unchanged.

## HM-V10-01 — A dependency becomes leverage, then receives a misleading account

Darkness's attempt to prevent diplomatic trouble physically binds Kazuma and promises comprehensive care. He exploits the resulting toileting problem, first presents it as unavoidable necessity, and subsequently calls the preceding activity enjoyable sexual harassment. Aqua's overheard fragments let him report the encounter as attempted molestation. This is a continuous reversal of who controls the practical situation, followed by control of the story about it. The bodily difficulty becomes real inside the scene without vindicating the coercive framing he adds. (C1 P0053–0149, especially P0088–0090, P0123–0132, P0139–0149.)

**H1:** the request for assistance, her protests and his admitted enjoyment are direct behavioral evidence. **H2:** the elaborate chamber-pot escalation amplifies an exploitable power reversal. **H3:** the exact morning bodily obstruction is an occasion-specific complication, not a stable rule that future restraint must produce the same result. Her near-tears/tearful-eyed descriptions must not be promoted into a proved actual-tear Gen03-K04 trigger; the continued harassment remains adverse to a broader refusal-sensitive or distress-sensitive character claim.

## HM-V10-02 — Useful capacity fails at the surrounding dependency

Aqua's assurance that her divine clothing blocks poison does not fail on its stated mechanism. She passes out drunk while the other three require the antidote she had promised, and the party subsequently seeks an Eris church. A godlike capacity is defeated by an ordinary availability problem. The conversion-by-poison proposal and the image of Aqua as an old man enjoying fin sake remain nested jokes within this meal's continuous safety premise. (C1 P0216–0271; explicit rescue account P0276–0279.)

**H1:** agreeing to provide antidotes, drinking and later absence from the needed service are relevant decisions. **H2:** the converging slurred speech and collective near-wipeout amplify the dependency failure. The text's poison immunity and magical cure are diegetic capacities, not H4 merely because they are supernatural. Local **L0** causal mechanism, with **L1** articulatory degradation and **L2** competence-only compliments (P0241–0243).

## HM-V10-03 — Protectors, teachers and experts are repeatedly displaced

Iris destroys the roadblock that the escort should handle; Darkness's portable mansion makes Kazuma's camping plan redundant; magical perception, water and concealment interrupt Megumin's fieldcraft lesson; Iris's dragon attack precedes Explosion. These are related role reversals with different causes, not evidence that every displaced person lacks ability. Kazuma still teaches cooking, Darkness later draws the dragon, and Megumin's explosive option is retained as a contingency. (C2 P0194–0261, P0264–0294; C5 P0046–0089, P0097–0126, P0147–0157.)

**H1:** role assignment and actual execution are strong competence evidence. **H2:** repeated claims that the guards are unnecessary amplify the social embarrassment. **H3:** the exact timing by which a particular specialist loses the finishing blow should not become a guaranteed rule. Megumin's stammering before the dragon and her retrospective insistence that she deliberately conceded credit must be read together (C5 P0103, P0150–0157).

## HM-V10-04 — Public dignity can conceal exploitation or ignorance

Ordinary fried rice, rice toppings and crayfish become discoveries for Iris and Darkness. Aqua's rudimentary meal wins deferential praise, while Megumin's initially distrusted crayfish is actually well prepared and enjoyed. The social rank of the eaters, the prestige of a food name and cooking ability are not equivalent. Separately, borrowed noble credentials coerce a teleport shopkeeper, stop an investigation and force continued casino play. Sentimental equality rhetoric does not remove the costs transferred to others. (C2 P0297–0445; C1 P0288–0321; C4 P0474–0481; C5 P0299–0309.)

The food sequences are chiefly **L0–L2**, with food-culture context separately flagged. **H1** covers witnessed preparation, tasting, payment and credential use; **H2** covers the repeated awe surrounding tiny domestic discoveries and the reach of status intimidation. Calling crayfish “lobster” and admitting that “secret recipe” was merely a phrase are character claims, not independent culinary facts (C2 P0383, P0444).

## HM-V10-05 — Outsourcing responsibility succeeds only at transferring its costs

The women and their suitors expect jealousy. Kazuma instead transfers the day's supervision to the men and escapes. The reunion supplies the costs: a sand-bath endurance casualty, destroyed farm stock and vanished expensive furnishings. His initial forecast that the companions would cause trouble gains support, but that does not excuse encouraging the men to accept responsibility, withholding what he anticipates, or joining the flight from reimbursement. (C3 P0052–0170, P0228–0230, P0254–0267, P0290–0323.)

**H1:** his reasoning and subsequent departure are explicit; the reimbursement plea is actually made and the group actually flees. **H2:** three tailored disasters compress each companion's preferences into an exaggerated result. **H3:** the exact trio and coincidence of their offscreen signals are comic construction, not a probability estimate. The feat of making furnishings disappear is not H4; the material bill preserves it as a real event, while the mechanism of the disappearance is not explained.

## HM-V10-06 — A reliable instrument is mistaken for an unrestricted truth oracle

The bell interrogation distinguishes sincerely held belief, deliberate deception, magical detection limits and social credibility. Darkness's refusal supplies her own family name; Megumin's beliefs fail to ring the bell; Aqua's true goddess claim is dismissed after several false claims also produced silence. Darkness explains the self-belief limit (C4 P0432), and Aqua explains her aura's interference and the exceptional conscience-paining lie that can still register (P0466–0470). The farewell references then ring repeatedly (P0496–0507).

**H1:** the bell sounds or remains silent, characters make specified claims, and the examiner changes conduct. **H2:** an escalating failure of reassurance makes Kazuma's defense socially worse. **H3:** exact ringing timing and the examiner's reactions are local outcomes. Neither silence nor sound independently proves every proposition in a compound utterance: the sequence does not verify Megumin's former-life identity, Aqua's guardian role, moral purity, or a globally accurate character assessment. (C4 P0349–0507.)

## HM-V10-07 — Wording can expose a real exploit without requiring Japanese-only translation

Kazuma asks where the coin is, allows the visible fists to imply two alternatives, and keeps the coin in a pocket. The prince's eventual recognition changes his view of the contests but does not force him to accept another political wager. The primary mechanism is a portable **L2** implicature exploit: “where” versus “which hand” preserves it. Exact words still matter. Levi later asks the fair binary version, 「コインはどっちにある？」; that is a serious reciprocal callback, not another joke merely because its wording is important. (C4 P0181, P0244–0284, especially P0263–0269; C5 P0459–0464.)

Kazuma's eventual casino plan replaces this trick with observed gambling wins attributed to his luck and institutional protection. His unseen-win summary does not prove that Eris actively intervenes, nor does Aqua's placement of a losing bet independently establish a measured causal effect of her luck on his. His explanations and the observed wins/loss must remain separate. (C5 P0269–0342.)

## HM-V10-08 — Familiar flaws become an authentication mechanism

The impersonator offers romantic speech while missing Kazuma's social position, agitation and gaze. Darkness recognizes the mismatch, yet enjoys a coercive fantasy until seeing that the real Kazuma witnessed it; when Iris is threatened, her response shifts to escape and protection. Kazuma's epilogue claim that only Iris detected the double is immediately corrected by Darkness. The joke uses stable interaction knowledge, not merely a visual resemblance. (C5 P0546–0579, P0612–0657; Ep P0024–0032.)

**H1:** detection, the hostage threat, requested wording, embarrassment and escape cooperation occur. **H2:** parody turns sexual unreliability into an identifying signature. **H3:** the precise impersonator errors are not a universal test for impostors. Apparent Megumin's earlier seduction is Ragcraft's performance and cannot become evidence of actual Megumin's willingness, voice baseline or consent (C5 P0488–0544, P0585).

## H4 and distribution calibration

The narration's declarations that time stops (C5 P0740, P0750), broad superlatives and impossible-to-count affective magnitudes are stronger **H4** candidates than literal spells, living vegetables, buffs or transformations. Local exaggeration qualifies intensity; it does not erase the actions it frames. Actual tears or pleading by a game opponent and casino staff are **H1** observations, while Kazuma's continuation blocks a generic “distress always stops him” conclusion (C3 P0207–0216; C5 P0283, P0299, P0384–0398). These are outside K04's valued-companion intimate/teasing conjunction, not favorable tests of it.

The **final audited V10 census contains 112 episodes/candidates: 106 clear L0–L2, 1 clear L3, 1 mixed and 4 uncertain**, with conservative local L0–L2 bounds **94.64%–99.11%**. The original 105-episode boundary-before-classification snapshot (SHA-256 `fe23a22171efd1b6c7953292a41d38b670bf8941f8eba5f5fe0982c435e0c429`) and its 101/1/1/2 distribution remain historical. A separate full audit of 96 uncovered ranges identified seven supplementary candidates; their boundaries were sealed before supplementary classification (SHA-256 `b848131ec2757e04b18d4189e9363b616974a5786343cb93d03fe29d5186c526`). Five add clear L0–L2 cases and two remain eligibility-uncertain. No original L class changed. Source-description corrections and all added IDs are recorded explicitly.

The complete source-order registry, cultural flags, ordinary-equivalent tests, original/final counts and near-miss decisions are in the canonical `../02 Sequential Readings/KONOSUBA_V10_DEEP_READING.md`, **Appendix A**; the fixed J01 comparisons are in **§8.4**. Short lexical/name jokes remain represented, and all four uncertain cases remain in the denominator. **MG03-J02 stays PARTIAL / TRANCHE_OPEN** until the complete V10–V12 pool is read and coded. Neither a local majority nor the coverage correction creates a new freeze or model generation.

## Recognition and role performance

V11's recurring sequence is recognition → role performance → overextension/exposure → audience response, with several distinct truth conditions. Court thanks rest on real achievement but license prolonged indulgence; paid fan letters create genuine motivation with manufactured provenance; Megumin's family reports exaggerate social treatment while invoking real feats; Luna redirects admiration into labor pressure; the Princess uses truthful fragments to support false impressions; Komekko reports conspicuous acts sincerely while losing explanations. Do not label every instance simple lying, vanity or spontaneous love. Pr P0035–0038; C1 P0142–0277; C3 P0038–0059/P0297–0444; C4 P0257–0494; Ep P0092–0103.

| Local mechanism | Source | Behavioral weight |
|---|---|---|
| Early-rising NEET, efficient servant/inefficient master, luxurious ingredients all subordinated to miso | C1 P0142–0214 | H1 selective effort and dependence; H2 self-importance/magnitude. |
| Genre refusal reinterpreted as consent; cake returned while he claims it does not count | C1 P0178–0214 | H1 motivated boundary interpretation; H2 spectacle. |
| Heroic resolution immediately defeated by affectionate address | C1 P0336–0389 | H1 changed decision and actual retirement; H2 rapid rhetorical collapse. |
| True missing memory supports a false heroic cause; insulting Aqua supplies the accurate cause | C2 P0094–0129 | H1 source/knowledge distinction; H2 comic confidence. |
| Private reunion plays against noisy external exorcism; apparent devil becomes radish | C2 P0293–0338 | H1 attachment/help; H3 particular prop gag, not unrestricted new power. |
| Real legal enforcement answers a genre retaliation manifesto; supplication reverses dominance | C2 P0411–0451 | H1 law/taunting/mercy with actual local K04 failure; H2 humiliation. |
| Supposed tea incompetence becomes intentional maid play, then healing restores embarrassing truth | C2 P0455–0534 | H1 intention, magical effect and delayed confession; H2 role performance. |
| A true flattering description makes planned confession unnecessary to its beneficiaries | C3 P0297–0400 | H1 selective endorsement and reciprocity; H2 boast escalation. |
| Peaceful soul release fails to deliver Aqua's prestige victory; a punch still loses the last word | C3 P0545–0579 | H1 motive/action/release; H2 bathos. |
| Public grace/private abuse and selective truth defeat a detector's naïve users | C4 P0257–0494 | H1 semantic/pragmatic inference; H2 sustained adversarial theater. |
| Food discipline is defeated by a kitchen barricade and hidden chocolate | C4 P0534–0580 | H1 incentive/resource reasoning; H2 timing. |
| Veteran nostalgia and grand one-on-one mastery face the actual two/three-enemy fight | F P0097–0330 | H1 tactics and constraints; H2 panic/sexual-threat farce, with harm retained. |
| Child's report poorly explains battle but precisely exposes private romance words | Ep P0092–0103 | H1 observer/causal distinction; H2 embarrassment. |

No important adverse behavior is excluded as H4 comic license: risky memory erasure, Darkness's collateral incapacitation, the collision and Kazuma's continued taunt all remain events. Transfer should preserve incentives, breaches and limits while recalibrating comic magnitude. The earned final praise is a serious resolution and is not separately counted merely because feeling changes. F P0344–0358.

The complete main narrative was read before the initial **119** unclassified episodes were sealed (`588b96b7addf071bbc2496a86101ef48d65114e5b5e5b91bd6fa0cf6fe1f4453`). The accepted conservative register118,661B SHA `00ed4164e5f52d30a0af0c9f5c2f3c03c47c3727e72c2eb58834f05d6b4e91e8` retains **127 clear L0–L2, zero clear L3 and three unresolved mixed cases**. Strict V12-only bounds are **127/130–130/130 (97.69%–100%)**. Zero consensus-clear L3 is not evidence that no source effect is form-dependent: H072 is independently coded L3 and held unresolved; nickname and name effects are retained. No fine histogram was forced:86 exact codes,44 fine differences,41 within L0–L2,3 scoring-group differences and2 strict clear-portable-status differences. Boundary discovery was preview-exposed in a limited way: later within-volume boundary anchors arrived when the producer was at C3P0410. No L information existed or was shared then. Source-ordered full reading and later independently sealed coding are still documented, but discovery is not called wholly blind. No denominator or boundary changed after classes. **MG03-J02 remains PARTIAL / TRANCHE_OPEN here**; formal pooling with unchanged V10/V11 belongs to the checkpoint.

These nested forms remain visible without adding episodes or changing the primary grouping after coding. Cultural context, comic eligibility strength, H weight and L dependence remain separate dimensions.

H4 caution: Kazuma's claim that a Japanese person dies without a daily bath is plainly nonliteral (C3P0066); a murder-suicide/revival retort is not a witnessed pair of deaths (P0409–0410). Neither establishes realistic physiology or routine violence. Genuine disease risk, burning and distress are not discarded as harmless comedy merely because adjacent scenes are farcical.

## V13 mechanisms — incompatible scripts and competing beneficiaries

The H tags concern **evidential literalness**, not translation. H1 means substantially usable direct action/motive/consequence in a comic scene; H2 preserves a disposition while bounding amplified magnitude; H3 marks a locally gag-dependent inference; H4 marks nonliteral comic license. A verbal joke can be H1 and language-sensitive; a spectacular physical scene can require H2 rather than literal realistic transfer. Source-present fantasy abilities are not automatically H4.

Vanir corrects, Wiz sincerely reframes, then actual beneficiary thanks her. | H1 for purchasing argument and care; do not merge commercial failure with global moral worth. The sincere flower is not a gag merely because it follows one. |

### Source-first denominator and coding authority

The final root-accepted boundary register contains192 units. Both original snapshots remain, with37 source-first operations net+23 from the primary169: additions, splits, resumed-run merges, repartitions and context repairs are individually attributable. All169 original IDs have lineage, all actual paragraph intervals are disjoint and native-text hashes match. Twenty-two whole units have soft salience before coding; this concerns whether a low-key barb or juxtaposition is independently salient, not whether its language will be transferable. Both original fine classifications must remain recoverable after comparison. The V13 census is a local contribution to Gen04's V13–V15 rule, not the full pooled tranche.

A descriptor clarification belongs outside any exact canonical table: inH034, the injured adventurer asks for **help** atC1P0387; Aqua ignores that plea while pursuing oreP0389–0390. The phrase about a plea and ore must not be read as a request for ore. This clarification changes no source coordinate, unit, class or count.

The root-accepted conservative register records **188 clear L0–L2, one clear L3(H030b), three MIXED_UNRESOLVED(H085/H112/H149b), zero uncertain**, yielding strict portable-count bounds **188–191/192**. The22 whole-unit soft eligibility cases remain inside that denominator; removing all22 produces the separately labeled sensitivity **166–169/170**, not a preferred replacement. The minor written eye-color case remains a clear L3 despite being brief. The three unresolved cases preserve competing whole-premise and nested-form readings: はぐれ/ハズレ, 芽/目 and the 勝負服 attire/duel hinge.

Both original complete fine-code/rationale/culture records survive in the joined register:121 exact fine-code agreements,71 differences, of which69 remain within the portable family. There are **two scoring-group differences** and **one strict clear-portable-status difference**; these are different quantities, not a formal reliability estimate. The broad versus granular culture schemes are not treated as comparable taxonomies. Cultural knowledge is separate from linguistic form: a genre or social script may need explanation while an ordinary equivalent preserves the principal causal joke. L0/L1/L2 distinctions remain disputed rather than forced into a consensus histogram.

# V14 Update

Source: admitted principal MAIN-14 only, all-body native paragraph locators. This append preserves all earlier ledger text and protected Gen04 originals. See V14 reading for full source identity, coverage, comparative provenance and qualifications.

## Comic mechanisms and evidential force

V14 repeatedly makes one practical or social meaning masquerade as another: a refuge is a friendship mission, a public performance is a heroic trial, a harmless door is a trap, equipment fit prevents the equipped skill, a hidden feat becomes proof to Yunyun and the present party in renewed rivalry, and an apparent saint is a disguised officer. These are different mechanisms rather than one universal reversal. Correct information can be rejected because its speaker has earned an unreliable reputation, and literally true statements can be engineered to imply a false event.

### Complete source-first census and bounded comparison

Both original uncoded discoveries were sealed before boundary exchange; root fixed the shared 154 whole-premise units before separate formal assignments and released complete code exchange only after both assignment seals. The complete appendix preserves both initial fine codes and rationales, lineage, source bounds, all eight scoped soft flags, current versus initial decisions and external source corrections. Root’s current accepted coarse grouping is 151 clear L0–L2 and three unresolved units (U028/U040/U082), with a descriptive primary-portability bound of 151–154 / 154. These are census bounds, not a statistical confidence interval or a claim that nested Japanese form transfers without adaptation. L1/L2 still require attention to phrasing and social stance.

The initial comparison remains 150 clear/four mixed and 109 exact fine matches/45 differences. U052 now has an explicit post-seal, postcomparison source reassessment: actual C2P0325–0338 has clipped replies and bodily euphemism, whereas the original repeated-negative claim belongs to U081 C3P0243–0244. Producer original MIXED_UNRESOLVED and independent original L2 remain intact; the later producer L1/current clear acceptance is not initial agreement or a negotiated shared fine code. Removing all eight soft-touched union units as a broad sensitivity analysis leaves 143 clear plus three unresolved / 146; primary eligibility and all boundaries remain unchanged. Full detail follows in the independently prepared report and appendix.

H1–H4 is not L0–L3. Source-world spells, armor effects and bodily harms remain actual within the fiction even when the genre exaggerates them. H2 limits transfer of intensity rather than erasing the action. H3 is appropriate where a one-off rhetorical device has little independent power to establish a durable trait. No broad H4 claim is needed merely because a scene uses supernatural mechanics.

# V15 Update — complete humor mechanics and paired census

Source: MAIN-15 admitted principal only, native EPUB SHA256 `ceae1226aa01421185231c837f542c430bd289489b73276b7086bb3c216ba005`. Root accepts this additive V15-local evidence through the V15 receiving review. Retained producer/candidate pending phrases describe their authorship boundary; publication and tranche adjudication remain separate. All prior ledger bytes, Gen04 originals and adverse histories stay exact. The V15 deep reading section13 routes every major claim family to H1–H4, shown/dialogue/report/inference access, qualitative confidence and governed claim transition. Apply those weights together with each row’s finer source/uncertainty limits; actual threat, refusal, pain and failed components are not erased by gag classification.

## V15 mechanisms and scope

The central comic engine is reciprocal constraint. Kazuma first tries to preserve safety by making a private deal, then repeatedly turns the enemy’s debt, reflection and control rules against her. Once the reversal works too well, the nominal master becomes a desperate provider and the nominal puppet makes himself expensive to command. The reversal is literary evidence about selected appraisals and choices; it does not make manipulation, pain or an expressed refusal unreal. The diary makes this particularly visible: it presents his altered valuation, documents actual coercive bargains and contains a separately marked invented erotic passage. The invented passage at C3P0029 is neither enacted contact nor Serena’s actual speech.

| Comic process / primary location | Setup, response and causal work | Literalness and model limit |
|---|---|---|
| Refuted household innocence, PrP0007–0018 | Aqua’s repeated denial encounters the practical challenge to use the seasoning; concession appears as a request for salt. The retort exposes accountability through a familiar meal. | Actual handling/taste disagreement; no medical service adoption merely because water/purification occurs. |
| Pet hierarchy turned on the household, PrP0019–0044 | The apparently subordinate creature attacks the claimed master; a peace interpretation ends with Aqua calling for help. | Actions and help call are real within the narrated scene. Species, observed fire and realistic damage require separate authority; Kazuma does not watch the fire instant. |
| Legendary villain versus precarious visitor, C1P0012–0153 | Rough speech yields courtesy, a reusable maiden story becomes confessed fabrication, military recruitment ends in a loan, and Kazuma punctures his own hero legend by crediting actual contributors. | New disclosures remain Serena’s claims; earlier readers did not already know the mechanism. The loan has later causal use. |
| Noble/legal frame punctured by household intimacy, C1P0185–0232; C2P0054–0113 | Darkness’s practical institutional help is accompanied by appearance planning and later public exposure of private history. The officer’s idealized image and Kazuma’s account collide. | Real legal labor and unwanted embarrassment coexist. Kazuma’s claim she is not angry is an interpretation, not consent or an acquittal. |
| Relationship wish no professional can provide, C1P0210–0223; C2P0217–0241 | The supposed universal consultant fails on the mundane shared-meal wish; later recruitment succeeds too quickly when Kazuma says only Yunyun can help. | The eventual role is real; loneliness cannot prove she endorses each tactic or that every proposed spell was cast. |
| Borrowed piety and public exposure, C2P0315–0375 | A praying pose becomes a mechanical robe-and-wire opportunity. Followers regain momentary clarity yet gather to hide her; a new crowd worsens the exposure and legal consequences. | Coercive stripping and her stop requests are actual. The custom Bind’s duration and comic crowd magnitude do not establish harmlessness or a general consent rule. |
| Specialist marketing defeats itself, C2P0589–0712 | Vanir prices the enemy’s entire purse, offers liberation goods, then Aqua converts stock into water to help Wiz. Enemies briefly laugh together before an insult recruits a separate fight. | Actual domain success destroys useful stock and holy water stings Wiz; later she is seen scorched during the fight, with the precise collateral route inferred by Kazuma. Product destruction precedes the divine insult and cannot be attributed backward to it. |
| The obedient puppet becomes the master’s burden, C2P0834–0958; C3P0006–0118 | Logical loyalty, debt preservation, commanded restraint removal and expensive compensation make Serena fund and endure the person she wanted as a tool. | Genuine altered allegiance coexists with discretionary exploitation. Tears, food deprivation, unwanted touching and threats are load-bearing limits, not erased by the reversed role. |
| Sacred certainty versus eclectic practice, C3P0131–0186 | Serena asks about exclusive faith; Kazuma describes mixed seasonal practice and innumerable gods, then offends and flatters her in adjacent turns. | Native ruby identifies 八百万 as やおよろず, “myriad”; no exact eight-million census. 無宗派 and 無宗教 retain their distinct lexical roles. |
| Explicit performance cue that fails as an instruction, C3P0271–0286 | A nonexistent resource name seems to explain failed control; Serena recognizes the real capacity problem, while repeated “do not resist” is treated as a comedian’s setup. | Capacity/release resistance is real. The source explicitly names the comic setup, so an unnamed specific comedy routine need not be invented. |
| Returned property is not restored agency, C3P0314–0443 | Companions complain that Kazuma has been returned in a broken state; the treasured gift becomes the actual cure. Megumin’s voluntary display adds a new relational response. | The gift’s function is unknown to Aqua at first. Serious restoration and possessive/rival attraction coexist; a cure does not retroactively justify earlier acts. |
| A threat defeats the speaker’s argument, C3P0524–0548; C4P0006–0137 | Darkness’s interrogation becomes reward through reflected pain; Kazuma’s solo-completion posture ends in death, familiar afterlife comedy and mediated bodily threats. | Reflection, death and threatened body interference remain real, but the exact edible object and full completed act are unshown. Eris reports the ground aftermath; she does not supply a stated observation mechanism. |
| Failed moral authority, C4P0234–0262 | Aqua condemns the laziness she shares, longs for the desperate debtor, and cannot sustain borrowed duty words under the others’ gaze. | The childish hiragana and fading phrase are exact written effects. Their whole-premise primary weight remains a genuine original mixed judgment; the broad hypocrisy is separately portable. |
| Stockpiled cure-all that nobody wanted, C4P0330–0346; FP0091–0114 | The confessed hoarder surveys the party and receives petty reasons for rejecting the quest; the shop’s powerful goods carry unacceptable costs or commercial bait. | The supplied survey sheet, its interviews and authorship are not independently verified. Fear of the very cold Neroido drink may invite a recognizable comedy echo, but a specific allusion is not source-certified. Asking for details at FP0114 is not a completed purchase. |
| Specialty pride and practical help share one destructive act, C4P0467–0520 | The reserve-ranked specialist destroys the shared recall letter, boasts first-team worth and then offers Yunyun excuses to stay; Kazuma’s accusation makes her flinch. | The first throw precedes the circulation warning; the later Explosion follows it. Friend support and status injury can coexist. Final town defense/stay and duty release are unshown. |
| Familiar teasing meets an actual attachment cost, FP0172–0373; EpP0003–0065 | Hunger prediction fails, moonlit beauty is punctured by familiar insults, a warning is instantly rejected, and a grand farewell ends in an erased request to be found. | Neither all-night confinement nor indefinite later insomnia is shown. Aqua’s written goal is real evidence of intent; her route, company, future fight and Kazuma’s completed pursuit are not. |

The H categories apply to claims rather than sanitize entire scenes. H1 covers literal selected acts, motives and consequences: assigning healing, resisting painful orders, paying for meals, unwanted exposure and a death followed by revival. H2 covers the amplified performance or magnitude of an otherwise causal pattern, such as a large public crowd or spectacular reciprocal pain. H3 limits stable generalization from gag-contingent performance or intensity; an actual body threat remains an act even where its threatened completion is unshown. H4 covers explicitly fictional, metaphorical or false content: the erotic passage, a wish-granting blue-tanuki comparison, the unverified grandiose billion-follower boast read as comic rhetoric, with Darkness’s divergent estimate kept distinct from an audited census, and false morning blame. A single unit can contain more than one level. The literal wrong done to a person survives alongside exaggeration and invented self-defense.

The complete uncoded originals sealed separately before boundary exchange, and root fixed all 150 whole-premise units before formal assignment. All 36 soft-touched units remain in the governed denominator, with original flags and scoped intersections. Complete separate assignments and paired receiving now preserve 149 clear portable L0–L2 units and one unresolved unit U15-120 under both initial histories; no original clear L3 is assigned. Exact fine-label strings agree on 106/150. The two differently spelled mixed labels express the same L1/L3 uncertainty, giving 107/150 only under that explicit normalization and leaving 43 portable fine differences. This is descriptive comparison, not consensus, human reliability or independent trials. The conservative lower bound is149/150, and broad wholesale soft-removal sensitivity is113/114; neither removes the mixed unit. The sole mixed premise concerns portable hypocritical moralism versus the written childish duty performance. It remains unresolved and included. V15 acceptance and pooled Gen04 adjudication are separate root decisions.

Whole-premise portability is separate from nested Japanese work. The title/manner mismatch, 「なんじ」 imitation, genital/name substitution, 「漢らしい」 graph choice, the holy-debt vocabulary and the final positive request against the expected negative letter formula can matter locally without making the entire causal episode untranslatable. Cultural practice and comedy echoes are separate flags. The complete paired appendix below retains every unit, both original rationales, all 289 original descriptions, boundaries/intersections, source errata and denominator rules. Heading-only presentation derivatives preserve every table/coded cell and all other source artifact bytes, with an exact inverse and 245-row embedding proof. No volume acceptance or translation experiment is claimed.

### MAIN-15 conservative humor census — candidate for root review

The complete initial paired comparison retains **149 clear portable L0–L2 units and one unresolved portable/L3 unit, U15-120**, out of all 150 frozen whole-premise units. Neither original assigns an unambiguous whole-premise L3. The unresolved row is not counted as clear L3 or silently made portable. The current source qualifications remain separate from the frozen rationale histories: the origin disclosure does not certify Vanir’s private intent; fivefold quoted price is not net profit; only Wiz promises the apparent child a cure; progress/sufficiency atC2P0374 is not an independently fixed numeric completion quota; minions address a present Vanir while complaining about earlier neglect; ring refusal precedes sentimental disclosure; the guild boast precedes actual teaching; and FP0051–0052 silences Kazuma and Megumin.
